Two changes (both builds, `es6` and `lib`): `Line.js` passes the final frame to `getVisibleLength`, and `useAnimatedLineLength.js` counts elapsed 1 as full. A third (2026-11-12, `LineDrawShape.js`): on a Line's first render its path is not drawn yet, so its length reads 0 and the draw-in wrote `stroke-dasharray: 0px 0px`, which the browser draws as a solid line — every line showed in full for a frame, vanished, then wiped in from the left (seen on every dashboard load); an unmeasured line now gets `0px 1px` (hidden). Still present in 3.11; drop the patch once Recharts fixes it, and re-check it on any Recharts upgrade (patch-package fails the install when it no longer applies). The deploy re-runs `npm ci` when `patches/` changes (`needs_ci` in `scripts/server-deploy.sh`). Separately, `LineChartPanel` keeps its plotted rows across refetches (`useStableChartRows`: unchanged rows keep their objects, a rebuild that changes nothing passes the same array).
- **Loading is not absence.** A server-stated absence (an explicit `null`, an empty list after load, a capability schema such as `movement_create`) still hides the subview; a pending query must not read as one. `period_returns` is `undefined` while loading and `null` when the server says none (`useGroupInfoConsolidatedTables`, `useAccountDetailPageData`); the account placeholder takes its category and title from the cached nav row / nav node (`placeholders/accountDetailPlaceholders.ts`) instead of the old `mutual_fund` stand-in, so category-keyed blocks mount right at first paint; the card strip mounts on a cold cache from `buildPlaceholderNavStripDash` (zero `card_metrics_by_slug` for every nav group node, so `requireNavCardMetrics` holds). Accepted pop-ins, because the client cannot know them before the bundle: the CC/mortgage sections and perf combos of a group that turns out to have none, the capability forms. The dashboard's `chart_shape` and the group pages' `chart_buckets` remain the nav-time hints for the patrimonio/perf panels and the grouped toggle, and `chart_coverage` (2026-10-20; `groupHostsLiabilitiesCoverage`, sidebar-nav cache v5) marks the Pasivos root so the coverage chart mounts from first paint instead of the composition chart swapping out when the payload lands.
- **Keep-previous-data is scoped to the entity** (`displayUnitQueryBehaviorFor(queryKey, entityKeyLength)` in `queries/displayUnitQueries.ts`): a unit, period, range, page or filter change holds the previous payload (the page dims it; `isPlaceholderData` now means held data of the SAME group/account/scope — **a held prior-unit payload is never fx-converted (2026-10-27):** a CLP↔USD switch shows the target unit's own react-query data when it is still warm, else the dimmed zero placeholders for charts and tables — and for the CARD STRIP the previous unit's cards, dimmed and in THEIR unit, which flip to the new values once they land (the dashboard from its held bundle; the group, Pasivos and account pages through `useHeldNavStrip`, the previous unit's nav context or stored snapshot, the account hero from its held detail row — `AccountDetailPageData.stripUnit`); the target unit's stored snapshot wins when it has one; a blank strip only when neither unit has anything. The nav snapshot and nav context queries are keyed on the unit (entity length 2) so neither holds a CLP payload under USD; the dashboard cards always animate (the old `unitSwitching` gate is gone); the client-side fx cache, `keepPrevBundleUnit.ts` and `synthesizeMissingUsd.ts` are gone), while a different group, account or daily scope starts from its placeholder — bare `keepPreviousData` had shown account A's bundle, undimmed, under account B's title. The ticker, watchlist and Flows P/L keep previous data across their unit/window changes too. Pages gate `notFound` / `loadFailed` / `<Navigate>` on the nav query having settled, never while it is pending.

## Rentabilidad benchmark row (2026-10-03; migration 207, `benchmarkLevels.ts`, `benchmarkComparison.ts`)

- **One definition of a period's % (2026-11-05; `periodPctDailyChain.ts`).** Every % the app shows is the scope's daily flow-adjusted `pct` (its own daily series) compounded over the period — a day is one step; 1W, a month, a year, YTD, the 1A/3A/5A windows and the all-time Total compound the days in them; a day with no return is flat, a period with none null. Readers: the Rentabilidad cells, 1D/1W included (`withDailyChainedReturns`, applied after `withShortHorizonCells`); the «Detalle por mes / año» rows of accounts (`monthlyPerfForAccountDisplay`) and groups (`withGroupDailyChainedMonthPct`, `detalleDailyChainedPct.ts`; a year is the compound of its months, so of its days); the dashboard account rows' `pct_*` (`withDailyChainedAccountRowPcts`) and the nav groups' `row_pct` / `parent_pct` (`withChainedGroupPct`, the accounts table and the value map's colours); the home class Δ chart's % view. Before, each surface applied the one-step `flowAdjustedPct` at its own grain — a whole month's or year's flow counted from its first day — so the card «Año», the Rentabilidad YTD and the detalle year disagreed whenever money moved mid-period. Kept on the one-step rule: a scope of liabilities only (its P/L is a cost, not a return; the Pasivos pages, card and mortgage rows) and the legacy group sub-tab. The old Total, P/L acumulado ÷ net deposits, rides beside the compounded one (`pct_total_on_deposits_*` on account rows, `on_deposits_*` on a group's total cell) and shows as the Total %'s tooltip. Cost: every non-liability account and portfolio group needs its full-history daily series in both units, so `rentabilidadDailyWarm.ts` warms all of them. **The chains keep only each day's pct (2026-11-09; `dailyPctSeries.ts`):** `groupDailyPctPoints` / `accountDailyPctPoints` / `dailyPctPointsForScope` build the full series uncached (its past part still comes from the history tier) and cache `{as_of_date, pct}` per day under the daily-series family (`daily.series|<scope>|<unit>|0|pct|…`); the full payload is built and cached only when a page's day view asks for it. Until then every % reader held the whole cached payload (every point's legs plus a value line per member) for all 48 scopes: the home bundle alone kept ~100 MB of heap per unit on the demo person, and the Render Starter instance (V8 sizes the heap from the machine's 512 MB: ~256 MB) died of «heap out of memory» 50 s after boot, in a restart loop, once the US chapter made the history longer — 98 → 56 MB per unit, the boot warm ~290 → ~200 MB; the warm's log line now prints the heap used and the limit, `scripts/profile-cache-memory.ts` lists what each warm step retains and the largest keys, and `render.yaml` starts the server with `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=320` (on the start command, not a service env var: that also reaches the build, and the client's Vite build aborted at 320 MB). Parity: `inversionesPerfPct.test.ts`, `dailyPctSeries.test.ts`.
- **The month windows (MTD → TOTAL) are chained from DAILY returns since 2026-10-04** (`periodReturnsDaily.ts`): each day's flow-adjusted `pct` of the page's own daily series (`resolveAccountDailySeries` / `resolveGroupDailySeries`, full history), from the month-end before the window's first month through today; the pesos and the window bounds stay the monthly rows'. Monthly chaining counted a month's deposits from its first day and misstated months with large mid-month flows (Acciones TOTAL 7,6% monthly vs 12,6% daily). The full-history daily series behind every Rentabilidad table are warmed in CLP after the page bundles (`rentabilidadDailyWarm.ts`: 36 scopes, ~11 s cold, ~1 s after a live-quote tick, one scope per macrotask).
- **Every Rentabilidad table (account pages, investment group pages, Inversiones) has a second row comparing it with a selectable benchmark**, default the mortgage, choice remembered per surface (`<pageKey>.returns`, `useSurfaceBenchmark`). Fetched apart from the page payloads (`GET /api/benchmark-comparison?benchmark=&unit=&account_id=|portfolio_group=`, `GET /api/benchmarks`), so a change of benchmark refetches only the row.
- **The row is a shadow portfolio:** for each window it starts with what the account or group was worth at the window start (1D/1W: the marks on the anchor day; month windows: the first month's `prior_closing`, the figure the real P/L starts from) and receives the same flows on the same days (`loadMergedDisplayDepositInflowEvents`, `flowEventInUnit` — transfers between a group's members cancel), all in the benchmark. Cells: the benchmark's own return over the window (L(end) ÷ L(start) − 1, comparable with the real time-weighted %), the shadow P/L, and Δ = the real cell's P/L − the shadow's. A window that starts empty measures from its first flow; a benchmark with no level at the start or on a flow day leaves the cell empty, never a guess. Levels are in the display unit at each day's rate (live fx today).
- **Benchmarks are rows of `benchmarks`, one per kind:** `equity_with_dividends` (`equity_daily` closes, each `equity_dividends` dividend reinvested at its ex-date close net of `withholding_pct`; SPY at 15%), `fund_unit` (a `fund_unit_daily` series; Risky Norris = `fintual_cert_risky_norris`), `index_plus_rate` (UF compounded at `rate_pct`: the mortgage at 4,95%, UF at 0, a DAP at 2% — migration 208). A level read on or before its date throws when the observation is over 10 days old (a stale series, not a weekend). A new benchmark of an existing kind is a row, not code. **`portfolio_group` (migration 209)** names one of the user's own groups (`portfolio_group_slug`; seeded Brokerage, Acciones, APV, Crypto): its level is the group's time-weighted return, each day's flow-adjusted `pct` from its own daily series chained (`chainDailyReturns`), in the display unit; offered under «Tus portafolios» in both selectors and only while the group exists. It is chained DAILY, so it differs from the group's Rentabilidad TOTAL, which chains monthly returns (every month's flows counted from day 1): Acciones 12,6% daily vs 7,6% monthly on 2026-10-04. In the prepayments comparison, payments that predate a benchmark's history are listed without an invested value and left out of both sides of the totals (`covered_payments` / `covered_from`).
- **`equity_dividends` is write-once** (Yahoo `events=div`): `stocks_nyse` fetches it with the closes for every NYSE ticker and benchmark tickers are always in that sync; a restated amount is a sync note, never overwritten. History: `npm run backfill:equity-dividends -w nw-tracker-server [-- --ticker=SPY --dry-run]`.
- Check: the SPY account against the SPY benchmark differs by tens of pesos over YTD/1A (ex-date vs pay-date reinvestment).
- **Both rows carry the money's IRR** (`real_irr_pct` / `shadow_irr_pct`, `irr.ts`): the rate that turns the window's starting value and every flow, on its date, into the end value — yearly on windows of 365 days or more, over the window on shorter ones (`irr_annualized`; annualizing a day is noise). Solved by a grid scan plus bisection; null when no rate or several rates fit (e.g. a shadow the same withdrawals leave below zero: Brokerage TOTAL against the mortgage). It replaced a Modified Dietz money-weighted %, which inflated on long windows with large flows both ways (826% on Brokerage TOTAL).
- **The mortgage page's «Prepagos vs invertir»** (`mortgagePrepaymentComparison.ts`, `GET /api/mortgage-prepayment-comparison?account_id=&benchmark=&unit=`) follows every peso paid above the minimum cuota (`loan_payments.extra_principal` on the `loan` rows: the `prepago` rows and the extra in each regular payment) to today two ways: prepaid, at the `mortgage` benchmark; invested the same day in the selected benchmark (default SPY, `account.<id>.prepayments`). Δ = invested − prepaid; totals carry both IRRs. Before tax; the insurance that shrinks with the balance is left out.

## Pasivos liabilities coverage (2026-10-04; total owed since 2026-10-20; `referenceCoverage.ts`)

- **On the Pasivos root page the composition chart's slot shows «Cobertura de los pasivos»** (`CoverageLineChart`): each chart-host reference line («Disponible», «Disponible total») ÷ the total owed on the same date — every liability account on the page (`isLiabilityKindSlug`: the mortgage balance plus the cards' owed-on-date) — as a percent with a dashed 100% line (where that money would clear every debt). Until 2026-10-20 the denominator was the mortgage alone (the cards are netted inside «Disponible» through cash, so they now sit on both sides — a deliberate choice: the chart reads «what I have against everything I owe»). A date without positive debt has no point. **The axis is capped at 200% (`COVERAGE_AXIS_CAP_PCT`):** the card-only years read in the thousands of percent and would flatten the mortgage era on a 0-anchored axis, so each line is DRAWN clamped to the cap (`|drawn` key) and runs along the top edge, with its clamped run overlaid in a darker shade of its colour (`|capped` key, `cappedStroke`) so the clamp is visible; the rows keep the true values under the series' own keys and the tooltip reads those (`mapPayload`). Not `allowDataOverflow`: without it Recharts would grow the domain back to the data, and with it the line simply left the plot. **In Diario it follows every chart's rule: its pending frame until its daily series lands** (see «A Diario chart never draws the monthly data» under «Loading states»; until 2026-11-04 it held the monthly block, dimmed).
- **Server-built at both grains, same block shape as the proportional ones** (`dates` + `series[].values`, fractions): the monthly group valuation payload's `reference_coverage` (from the block's own reference lines and liability lines, `liabilitiesCoverageFromBlock`) and the daily series' `reference_coverage` (from `reference_lines` and the liability accounts' daily lines). Data-driven: emitted only where the page hosts reference lines AND holds a liability account, so Efectivo (which hosts «Tarjeta de crédito») and the sub-pages keep their composition chart. It reuses that slot's Período/Rango (`liab.<slug>.proportional`).

## Daily series — calendar-day grid deltas (`dailySeries.ts`)

- **`getBucketDailySeries(accounts, { unit, days, now?, includeAccounts? })`** (`server/src/dailySeries.ts`): one point per **Chilean calendar day** ending today (`chileWallClockAt(now).ymd`; `days: 0` = since `portfolioStartYmd`, `DAILY_SERIES_MAX_DAYS` 7400 is a sanity bound, not a product cap — full-history daily is a user choice, 2026-07-21). Values are true levels via `bucketValueInUnitAt` (→ `accountMarkClpAtYmd`) — each account's mark is flat on its own closed days (weekend/holiday), so **per-account market-calendar attribution falls out of level deltas** (a `.SN` line moves on Chilean days, USD stocks on NYSE days, crypto/UF every day) with no session-window bookkeeping. Each point carries **`market_day`** (`isNyseTradingDay || isChileBusinessDay`; false = weekend/shared holiday — the detalle table dims those rows). Flows use the same deposit-event accounting as `netDepositFlowBetween`, bucketed per `(prev_day, day]` (bulk `loadMergedDisplayDepositInflowEvents` — USD-cash included since 2026-08-04). `pl = delta − flow`; missing legs → null, never a fake 0. Grid helper: `chileCalendarDaysListEndingAt` (in-file; the old `nyseSessionsListEndingAt` was removed).
- **Money without a value gives no return (2026-10-04; `unvaluedMemberDays`).** A bucket sums only the members that have a mark that day, so a member whose money arrived before its first valuation (the AFP's 2017-06-12 contributions, valued from 2017-06-30) read as the money in without its value: −79% on 06-12 and +358% on 06-30 on Retiro/Inversiones, which collapsed a daily-chained TOTAL to 3,55%. A run of unmarked days that money entered and that ends in a mark — and the day the mark appears — now has `pl` and `pct` null (flat in a chain). Never-valued (not yet open) and no-longer-valued (closed) members are not flagged.
- **Liquidation-edge conventions (2026-08-04):** (1) **USD-cash deposits are event-based, native-frame** — `loadForeignCashCapitalSortFlows` (`equityBrokerageCapitalFlows.ts`) emits every balance-affecting non-interest movement as a deposit event (USD sign = `signedNativeDeltaForAccountMovement`, so Σ event USD ≡ balance − interest by construction); the CLP frame is per event, never a re-priced aggregate: compra counter legs at their real pesos, equity legs (`stock_buy`/`stock_sell`/`dividend_payout`) mirror the stock side's attribution exactly (intra-bucket transfers net to the peso), legless USD at the event date's sell rate. **A wire shared by same-day buys is split pro rata (2026-09-28; `wireProRataFlows`):** with exactly one CLP→USD conversion on the cash account that day, no buy equal to it, and the day's buys needing at least its dollars, each buy carries `wire pesos × buy / Σbuys` at face plus the dollars the wire did not cover (USD already held, e.g. a swept dividend) at the reference rate — the last buy takes the rounding, so the pieces add up to the wire; one buy larger than the wire is the old wire + residual composite. Before, two buys from one wire both fell to the reference rate while the wire came in at its real pesos, so the conversion spread read as a −100% month on the cash account (Racional USD 2026-07) instead of sitting in the stocks' cost. The old balance−interest re-pricing (flowsDeposits window deltas, valuationTimeseries dep maps, dailySeries telescoping, `usdCashMonthlyPerfRows` netFlow) is gone — a no-event window reads 0 deposits in both frames; fx moves on held USD land in P/L like equities. CLP ledger cash and checking accounts follow the same rule since 2026-09-27: the group consolidation sums their merged deposit events per month through `ledgerCashNetFlowBetween` (events load once per account via `netDepositFlowWindows`), and `valuationTimeseries` has no CLP-cash balance − interest branch any more, so the aportes line, both performance tables, the card and the daily series read the same events. In pesos the events sum to balance − interest on every day, so only the USD frame moved: fx moves on pesos held are USD P/L (the consolidated checking P/L used to read 0 in every closed month). These monthly rows (USD cash included) give the first month P/L = close − flow, as the account performance builder does. (2) **`pct_month` is sign-guarded** (`flowAdjustedPct(nominal, prior, netFlow, close, eps)` in `periodReturns.ts`, `flowAdjustedPctMonth` until 2026-09-27 — accountPerformance's closed months, live current-month row and first months, both consolidations, the dashboard pcts (closing at the row's current value), the daily series `pct`, the 1D/1W cells and the equity position's return on deposited all use it). **A period that ends at zero** (`|close| ≤ eps`) on a net withdrawal divides by `prior` (2026-09-27): the start frame's `prior + netFlow` is then exactly `−nominal`, so every sale that emptied an account read −100% however small the loss; withdrawing exactly the prior close reads 0%, ending at zero with no prior close (bought and sold inside the period) reads null, and ending at zero on a net deposit keeps −100% (everything at work was lost, never below −100%). **Zero is the unit's coin** (`ZERO_CLOSE_EPS`: 1 peso, 0,01 USD, 0,0001 UF; `flowAdjustedPct` takes the unit) on every grain: a close, prior or start base at or below it is no capital, so a one-peso dust balance reads null instead of dividing the day's fx move by a peso. **Group periods carry each member's zero close** (`endChargedFlow` / `groupStartFrameFlow`): a member that ends at zero on a net withdrawal has it charged at the period end, capped at the group's own net withdrawal so a transfer between members is not counted twice — in `consolidateGroupMonthlyPerf` (rows carry `end_charged_flow`, summed by the yearly rollup), the net-worth / inversiones bucket sums (re-capped), the daily series and the 1D/1W cells; a period where no member empties is unchanged: when `prior + netFlow ≤ eps` (a liquidation withdraws intra-month gains too) the denominator falls back to `prior` — otherwise a close-at-0 month reads exactly −100% by the identity `nominal = −(prior + netFlow)` and poisons every chained Rentabilidad window. (3) **Daily chart lines clip as units** (`trimDailyLineEdges`): a never-held-in-window line nulls its `deposits_acum` too, aggregated bucket lines re-trim after summing, and the daily payload carries **`chart_end_ymd`** (mirror of the monthly block's) when every account line ended — the client trims valuation-chart points only (`buildDailyValuationBlock`); `points` stay full-length for P/L bars and the detalle table. (4) **Grouped bucket aportes aggregate from PRE-trim deposits (2026-08-08):** `groupDailySeriesAccounts` sums each bucket's `deposits_acum` from the series' internal `deposit_cums_raw` (every member's raw cums — wound-down and never-held-in-window included — then the aggregate edge-trims as a unit), never from the display-trimmed member lines: an ACTIVE bucket with a liquidated member (brokerage after the caca daca wind-down) otherwise loses that member's terminal net deposits at its clip day, jumping the hub's aportes line by the dead member's withdrawals (+21M on /inversiones). A bucket line's terminal aportes now equal that bucket's own leaf-page `deposits_acum_total` by construction; `deposit_cums_raw` is stripped from HTTP payloads at the `/api/daily-series` route.
- **One conversion for deposit and flow events (2026-09-27; `flowEventInUnit`, flowsDeposits.ts).** Every merged-deposit reader (dashboard totals, `netDepositFlowBetween`, the flows deposits page, monthly/daily aportes, the daily grid incl. mortgage and derived card flows, the fx scan) and the depto ledger payments (mortgage card, performance table, daily view — the property's payments too) convert each event at its own date's rate: a carried leg as is (CLP `amt`, a recorded `amt_usd`), CLP→USD at the buy rate on/before the date (`clpToUsdAtPayment`, unrounded), CLP→UF at that day's UF. Unrounded; a missing rate throws, never a skipped event or a null total, and the flows deposits payload has no `fx_conversion_error`. The performance table's USD mortgage/property flows used to convert the month's sum at the month-end rate, so they disagreed with the card and the daily view (mortgage all-time 1xx.xxx,xx vs 1xx.xxx,xx USD). Test fixtures a USD reader converts must sit inside the test person's market window (about two years back). Summary «Depositado», chart aportes and dashboard deposits all read personal capital (`movementCountsAsPersonalDeposit`: APV-A's state bonus is not a deposit). Not yet on the rule: the deposits reconciliation, and UF window flows (converted at the window end, the ledger-cash group tables included). A CLP event converts at the buy rate while balances use the mid, so on a cash account a deposit reads a small +USD P/L in its month and a withdrawal a small −P/L; they cancel over a round trip.
- **Group consolidations get each account's payload whole (2026-09-27).** Both paths into `consolidateGroupMonthlyPerf` (the cached group consolidation, which also feeds net worth, inversiones and flows P/L, and the group tables' `consolidateFromAccountMonthly`) used to rebuild the payload field by field and drop `import_key`, so a Fintual certificate account's month-end mark fell back to its perf rows — already in the requested unit — and the USD path divided them by fx again: every USD group % containing a Fintual account was off (up to absurd values on the APV and mutual-fund groups), while CLP was right. Its latent twin is gone (2026-10-28): `monthEndCloseForAccountInUnit` converts only the mark (`monthEndMarkClpForAccount`) and takes the perf-row fallback as it is, already in the rows' unit; it also converts to UF now, and the live current-month row reads UF (close at today's UF, the month's flow at today's UF) — both had returned pesos in a UF series.
- **Cached wrapper** `getBucketDailySeriesCached(scopeKey, …)` (real clock only) → `daily.series|<scope>|<unit>|<days>|<ids>` keys, dropped by `invalidateCcBillingDetail` (the account/CC write funnel) and `invalidateMarketDataAggregations` via `invalidateDailySeries()`.
- **Book-value carry for manual-marked accounts** (`storedMarkFlowCarry.ts`): the terminal stored-`valuations` forward-fill (historical branch in `accountMarkClpAtYmd` + both generic stored returns in the `accountTodayValue` stack) returns stale mark **+ net personal flows since its date** (`netDepositFlowBetween`, the same reference as the daily/d1 flow legs) — so a deposit day steps the value with `pl = 0` by construction, and the next mark day carries the clean inter-mark P/L. A mark dated the flow day has an empty carry window (no double count). **Never applied to CC or mortgage balances** (kind-gated: CC owed-on-date has its own derivation; a mortgage payment's balance effect is amortization only). This also makes the Rentabilidad d1/w1 cells and today's dashboard values deposit-aware for these accounts. Same pass fixed the today stack's ordering: depto property/mortgage UF marks resolve **before** the stored return (first in `accountTodayValue` since 2026-10-28) — a stale property valuation no longer shadows the live ledger mark in allocation pies/live points.
- **Known limits:** monthly-marked accounts (DAP, cuenta ahorro) still render P/L as steps — the mark day carries the accumulated inter-mark P/L (carry only moves flows, never fabricates intraperiod returns). Cold-build costs (real DB, 2026-07-21): 90d ≈ 1.3s, 1y ≈ 2.1s, full history (~3.7k days × 31 accounts) ≈ 19s. The depto property/mortgage ledger is memoized (`depto.ledger|rows`, dropped by `invalidateCcBillingDetail`); remaining hot spot is CC `latestCreditCardValuationRowAsOf` (~400ms on ·0161).
- **The account page header shows the dashboard card's value (2026-09-26; one function since 2026-10-28, `accountTodayValue.ts`).** Both read `accountTodayValue` — the value `accountMarkClpAtYmd` returns at Chile today, every dashboard row, liabilities included, so today shares a frame with the prior-day and month-end marks — which carries one override: accounts valued from units × price (cuota ledgers, Fintual cert funds, crypto) show their position's mark, the position asked at TODAY. The header used a second ladder, `latestValuationDisplayForAccount` (deleted), without the carry or the depto branch: the depto property header showed a stale stored valuation 3,xx M below its card.
- **Per-account mark cache + live-tail re-pricing (2026-07-23):** marks dominate a daily build, so each account's mark series is cached on the calendar-day grid (`server/src/accountMarkDailyCache.ts`, `accountMarkClpSeriesOnGrid`; the cells are quantities, see «The daily cache stores quantities») and the daily consumers — `getBucketDailySeries`, `buildDashboardBucketDailySeriesClp`, the overview payload's liabilities leg — read it instead of calling `accountMarkClpAtYmd` per account per day. **Only days strictly before Chile today are cached**; today always recomputes (live quotes, intraday fx). That split is what makes the live-quote poll cheap: no historical mark reads `live_market_quotes` (`accountMarkClpAtYmd`'s live stack is gated on `asOfYmd === today`, `computeEquityMtmClp` only reaches `fxForLiveMtm` when handed an explicit live price, and `computeCryptoMtmClp` reads `fxForLiveMtm(asOfYmd)` whatever the price source — a historical date never matches the live row's session, so it reads `fx_daily`), so a live tick drops the daily *aggregations* and keeps the marks. The series **extends** in both directions as wider windows are requested (a 90d view pays 90 days; a later Total view pays only the missing prefix), in their own store (`markSeriesStore.ts`); grids must be contiguous ascending calendar days (checked O(1), throws) and a length/range mismatch throws rather than silently shifting values by days.
- **Mark-cache waterfall (2026-10-05; migration 211, since F2 `markInputTriggers.ts` + `cacheRegistry.ts`).** The per-account mark series live in their own store (`markSeriesStore.ts`), not the aggregation cache, so they survive the Chile day rollover (a past day's mark does not depend on the date it is read) and every write that does not touch an account's inputs. Each table a historical mark reads has a trigger spec (movements, valuations, the card statement / line / installment / traspaso tables, mirror merges, loan payments, fx (+ bid/ask, observado), UF, fund units, equity closes, the proxy basket, accounts, asset/portfolio groups, market symbols) logging `(account or NULL = every account, raw date)`; the registry trims each affected series to the days before the change by the spec's rule (`backDays` / `recentDays` / `rateTable`: a card's dollar lines read the fx of their pay-by, so an fx row reaches 75 days back for cards; a statement header its billing period; the proxy's observado 7 days; a fund cuota 10 days; the proxy basket 30 days) and drops the daily aggregations and input memos built on them. An equity close logs its ticker's holders only (every account when the ticker is in the proxy basket; nothing for a watchlist-only ticker). The depto property and mortgage accounts are trimmed together (`declareAccountsTrimmedTogether`). A movement that is its account's first units row logs the whole history (it switches the valuation method). `clearAggregationCache()` still drops everything, marks included. A source with no spec throws.
- **A live-quote tick rebuilds only today (2026-10-30; the history and live tiers in `cacheRegistry.ts`).** A tick still drops the page bundle, the monthly-perf / consolidation / flows payloads and the daily aggregations, but what they are built from survives it: the **history tier** (`historyTierMemo`, the `hist.` family) memoizes every input a live quote cannot move — a past day's `accountMarkClpAtYmd` (the month-end marks every monthly builder walks; the daily mark store keeps its own days and bypasses it), the merged deposit timelines (`buildMergedDepositMap`), the ledger month closes before today (`balanceOnlyMonthlyRowsAsc`), equity / crypto MTM at a past day (`computeEquityMtmClp` / `computeCryptoMtmClp` without a live price or `now`), the linked-card netting by past day, a past day's USD / UF rate and USD-cash dollars in the daily series (`pastDayUnitConverter`). It is dropped wholesale by every other invalidation — a write row of any logged table, `historical` market data, a mark row of any connection, the day rollover and `data_version` — so outside a tick it is as fresh as the rest of the cache; a new entry may read only mark-input tables or rows one of those funnels covers, never a live quote, the live fx row or the clock beyond the Chile day. Inside a transaction both tiers hand out throwaway maps (report-only imports read rows they roll back). The **live tier** (`liveTierCached`) memoizes `accountTodayValue` on the real clock for the rest of the UTC minute — it was asked hundreds of times per rebuild — and is dropped by every invalidation, a tick included. The wall clocks (`chileWallClockAt`, `nyseWallClock`) are memoized per UTC minute (minute resolution, whole-hour offsets, so exact). A tick now also drops the cached card billing-detail bundles (`cc.billing_detail|<id>`, not their satellites): an unpaid facturación prices its dollars at the live rate (`usd_rate_source: live`), and the «próximos pagos» panel had served the rate of cache-build time. Byte-identical by test: `liveTickIncrementalRebuild.test.ts` (synthetic DB — no stock accounts, so the live fx is the mover — frozen trading afternoon) and the same 308 payloads on a real-DB copy — bundles, flows overview, overview-daily, every group's daily series / consolidation / valuation TS and every account's monthly perf, CLP and USD — equal to a rebuild from an empty cache after two simulated ticks. **The flows overview's inputs follow the live dollar rate, not the tick (same date; `syncFlowsInputsWithLiveUsdRates` in `flowsMortgageCarrying.ts`):** the expense lines price an unpaid facturación's dollars at that same live rate, so the inputs (`flows.overview.inputs|`) record the live rates they used (the cards' facturaciones with `usd_rate_source: live`) and are dropped when a reader finds the cards' current ones differ — a tick that moves only stocks or coins, or one after the fx day closed, keeps them. The lines rebuild in ~1,6 s instead of ~5 s (the receipt matcher's day arithmetic is memoized and windowed by day, the display dedupe buckets purchases by account/facturación/pesos/day instead of scanning every kept line, the nota-cancelled plan ids sit in the history tier), checked identical on a real-DB copy but for the synthetic gap-mirror ids, which change on every build anyway. Warm after a tick on a real-DB copy (M-series, loaded machine): **~19,4 s → ~3,5 s** with the live fx moving (bundles 7,4 + 8,1 → 1,1 + 1,0 s, flows 0,6 → 1,0 s, Rentabilidad series 3,3 → 0,4 s), ~2,8 s when only stocks or coins move; the cold warm went 55 → 35 s. The parity test covers the flows overview too. Not done: trimming the history tier by date on `historical` invalidations (the mark-series waterfall's rule) instead of dropping it.
- **A dropped payload rebuilds only today's part (2026-10-31; `docs/performance-plan.md` «A-F as built»).** On top of the tiers above, the expensive builders keep their past in the history tier: the daily series' past part (`daily_series_past` in `dailySeries.ts`: each account's value in the unit on every grid day but the last, the flow legs, deposit cumulatives and market-day flags), the ledger accounts' closed-month rows (`ledger_closed_month_rows`), a plain account's raw valuation-chart mark on a past date (`valuation_chart_past_raw_clp`; never the trailing or today point, never the card / mortgage / property lines), a card's statement list (`cc_statements_by_account`, frozen rows) and the deposit-event buy rate (`fx_buy_rate_on_or_before`, rates found only). A card's billing detail is two cached parts: the base (`cc.billing_detail|<id>|base`: ledger payload, detalle, facturaciones without their dollar rates), which a scoped tick drops only when it read a moved price, and the bundle, which copies the facturaciones and applies the rates (`withFacturacionUsdRates`), so a dollar tick re-applies only the rates. Each Pasivos reference source's totals are a cached group-response part (`reference_source_totals`). **`buildHistoryTierValue(what, build)`** (`cacheRegistry.ts`) runs a history-tier part in its own dependency node and throws if it read any live input (a quote, the live dollar, or a gate deciding whether to read one) — use it for new parts; the valuation-chart memo is the one exception, because the equity MTM consults the live-dollar gate even for a past day (it reads the stored close there; `equity_mtm_clp` relies on the same rule). Parity: `liveTickIncrementalRebuild.test.ts` runs a coin-only tick on a Bitcoin account it adds, besides the dollar ticks.
- **Group/account page per-account lines: plain stored-valuation accounts use the mark (2026-07-24, D↔M convergence 2b):** `buildPointsForAccounts` forward-filled the last stored `valuations` row onto later chart dates with no flow carry, so a deposit or liquidation after the last mark left the per-account line stale (the daily view winds it down via `storedMarkValueWithFlowCarry`). Plain stored-valuation accounts — manual marks, DAP, cuenta ahorro, the pre-Fintual «Principal» predecessor accounts — now value each chart date through `accountMarkClpAtYmd` (stored + net flows since; null before the first mark), matching the daily series. **Guarded to plain-stored only**: equity/crypto/cash MTM, credit_card, mortgage, afp, property, and Fintual cert keep the raw their own branches compute above (those special framings are intentional — AFP ledger×px transit months, the Fintual cert freshness gate). Verified on the real DB: the change touched exactly one account (the newly-split «caca daca — principal», winding down 2018-03→06 instead of sitting at 2.09M), nothing else. **Fintual cert accounts now read the live mark too (2026-07-24):** the group-page branch used to prefer the stored `valuations` snapshot on historical dates (`fintualCertValuationRawClpForChart`, now removed), which lagged the true fund value when the snapshot sync was stale; they were folded into the mark-valued set, so the line is cuotas×fund-unit-price (the certificado value) at every date. With the values redundant, the **stored Fintual `valuations` rows were deleted** (300 rows, real DB, 2026-07-24) — `augmentChartDatesForFintualCertAccounts` now derives their chart date grid from the cuota history (month-ends from the first cuota event, which must include **transfer legs**: aportes are mostly modeled as checking→fund transfers, so a single-leg-only MIN would miss the start). Verified byte-transparent before deleting: with the augmentation active, dropping the rows left every surface identical. **Edge trims on per-account lines (2026-07-24):** both grains null each line's **leading** run of 0/null so a late-starting account begins at its first holding (equity already read null pre-holding; crypto's zero-unit mark is a finite 0 — this makes them agree), and the daily additionally mirrors the monthly's **trailing** clip (`trailingZeroRunClipStartIndex`, shared out of `timeseriesTailClip.ts`): a sold-out account keeps one plotted zero — one DAY at this grain vs one month-end at the monthly's — then ends, with `deposits_acum` clipped alongside its value exactly as `applyTrailingZeroTailClipToBlock` bundles them. `primary_lines` on the overview-daily payload gets the same trailing clip (its monthly twin is block-clipped). **Display-only**: bucket `points` (the P/L legs, which read the untouched `wealthByAccount`) keep their true zeros. Verified on the real DB: leading flips 0, trailing 254 → 3, **0 real value changes**. Residual by construction: the monthly's kept zero lands on a month-end and the daily's a day earlier, so one cell per sold-out account still reads monthly-0 vs daily-null at month-end comparisons. **AFP folded in too (2026-07-24):** it had the same shape — stored snapshot for history, live mark only on the trailing point — but its historical rows are a **write-once cache no scheduled job refreshes** (the `afp_uno` sync only upserts a today spot row via `upsertAfpSpotValuationWithExplicitPx`; `revalueAfpAccountFromCuotas` is manual (its script, `afp-uno-apply-valuation`, is in `server/scripts/archive/`)). They had gone stale against the 2026-07-10 cert-backed ledger rebuild: of 141 rows, 69 agreed with the mark, **42 were stale** (their implied cuota count was the pre-rebuild one), 2 were the documented AFP-switch transit months, and **28 predate the price series**. Fix was ordered — **revalue first** (113 rows rewritten, the 28 pre-price rows correctly skipped → 113 agree), **then** converge the chart, which made the convergence a verified **no-op** on every group line instead of shifting 42 historical points. Since 2026-10-22 each fund manager is its own account with its own official series (see «The AFP account's ledger and price»), so ledger × price values every date and the stored rows are re-valued from it (they still carry `units_snapshot` readers). `afpValuationRawClpForChart` is gone; the live last point is patched by `patchLiveLastPoint` in `valuationTimeseries.ts`.
- **Dashboard «Patrimonio neto» daily mode + the USD milestone lines (2026-07-24, phase 3; milestones moved 2026-10-27; «Cuentas principales» chart removed 2026-10-31):** in Período Diario the home net-worth chart swaps to the daily series. The «Cuentas principales» chart (per child group of brokerage / retirement + cash) was removed 2026-10-31 with its payloads (`accounts_ex_property`, the overview-daily `primary_lines`, the chart shape's `primary_lines`). **The USD milestone reference lines (US$50k … 500k) sit on the «Patrimonio neto» overview chart** (the separate «Patrimonio neto vs invested (referencia US$)» chart and its `patrimonio_usd_milestones_chart` / daily `patrimonio` blocks were removed 2026-10-27): the home overview block (`withUsdMilestoneReferences`, home builder only — nav pages keep the plain overview) carries `usd_*` fields on every point, the five `reference` lines and `referenceMilestoneByDate` for the client's leading anchors, and each overview-daily point carries the same fields (`milestoneFieldsForDate`). Milestones are the USD amount in the request unit — flat in USD, US$ × the day's fx in CLP, and a future unit with a direct USD rate (EUR/USD) converts in `usdMilestoneLevelInUnit`, never crossing through CLP. The client draws them faint (`isFaintReference`, the `dashboard-overview` color plan, keyed on `level_currency`): they never stretch the Y axis and are clipped to it, stay out of the legend and take no hover (no hit line, so hovering never highlights them); each carries `level_currency: "usd"` (the currency its level is fixed in), and the tooltip lists it only when the chart shows another unit — in USD its value would repeat its name; the chart-shape skeleton carries them too, so loading and loaded draw the same lines. Because phase 2a made the monthly totals mark-based, the daily and monthly overview agree to the peso at month-ends (verified in `dashboardOverviewDaily.test.ts`).
- **One home Δ chart, stacked by class (2026-10-27).** «Δ mensual y depósitos por clase (inversiones)» stacks the retiro + brokerage P/L (class colours) and, beside it, the retiro + brokerage deposits (the same hues at lower HSL saturation, `desaturateStroke`), with «Σ Δ mensual» and «Σ depósitos» lines over the two stacks; the YTD area always shows, and «Total acumulado» adds the all-time P/L (`accumulated_earnings`) as a solid line. The separate «Δ mensual y depósitos» chart and its MA3 lines were removed. The whole combo is served (2026-10-28, see «One month series per account»): the page bundle's `inversionesPerf` and `GET /api/dashboard/inversiones-perf-daily`; deposits by class come from `inversiones_deposits_chart` (monthly) and the two daily series' own deposit legs (day). `MonthlyPerformanceComboChart` takes a bar `stackId`: stacked bars draw with `stackOffset="sign"`, the Y axis sums each stack's positive and negative parts, and the bar band counts one slot per stack; charts without a stack id are unchanged. **A «%» toggle (2026-11-05; surface `home.perf`)** switches it to returns: each class's own return per period as side-by-side bars (`pct_retirement`, `pct_brokerage` — they do not add up, so they do not stack), Inversiones' return as the Σ line (`pct_combined`), YTD % as the area (`ytd_pct`), all-time % for «Total acumulado» (`accumulated_pct`), no deposits. All from the one rule above, server-built on every point, plus `yearly_pct` on the monthly block (one point per calendar year: a year's % is a compound, so the client takes it instead of rolling months up; a Rango clips whole years in this view).
- **Monthly dashboard chart totals = marks at month-ends (2026-07-24, D↔M convergence):** the dashboard overview chart values each chart date by **Σ `accountMarkClpAtYmd`** at that date (`slugMarkTotalsAtDatesClp` in `server/src/dashboardChartMarkTotals.ts`), the same marks the daily views sum — so a monthly chart value at date `d` equals the daily chart value at `d` by construction. `buildDashboardPortfolioGroupTotalsClp` still reads the consolidated monthly closings for **date discovery only** (which month-ends/snapshot dates are chart points — the P/L detalle tables keep using them) and then re-values those ~130 dates through marks; the legacy `mapMonthlyClosingToChartDates` forward-fill and `liabilitiesBucketTotalByDates` were dropped from this path. Overview slugs (real_estate/retirement/brokerage/cash_eqs/liabilities) reproduce the daily overview builder exactly (same accounts — the two resolution paths were verified account-identical — same cash CC-netting, same rounding). The **headline/cards path is unchanged** (`apportionedBucketClpAt`/`consolidatedBucketValueClpAt`) and still equals the chart's last point because both value today through marks. Cost: marks are read at the ~130 month-ends only (NOT a full daily grid — that made the build 22s), so the dashboard TS cold build is ~2,5s (was ~1,6s), warm ~0,7s. Verified on a real-DB copy: overview + patrimonio agree with the daily series to the peso (0 differing cells); historical corrections are stale-CC-netting at 6 month-ends (e.g. 2019-12 cash −269k — that one was the old clamp dropping the retired ·0161's credit, undone 2026-09-26, see «One cash netting rule») and a mid-month snapshot date (2026-06-08) previously showing June's month-closing instead of that day's mark.
- **One cash netting rule (2026-09-26): `cashNetOfLinkedCreditCards` (`cashEqsBucketNet.ts`) is the only place the linked-card total enters a cash_eqs value** — the live card and its prior closes, the daily series, the monthly chart totals, the group consolidation and the daily reference overlays (the client never re-sums it, and never converts it: a placeholder is always its own unit's `nw_bucket_totals`). Signed, never clamped: an overpaid card owes a negative amount, so a linked total in credit adds to cash, as Pasivos already counts it as negative debt; the linked-balance footer shows it (hidden only at 0). Unrounded; callers round once at the end. The old `applyCashSavingsNwAdjustment` clamp dropped a credit on the mark paths while the consolidation counted it, so the live card and its prior-day close differed by the credit (a phantom day change). On the real DB this moved 27 days in 2018-02 → 2021-02 (·0161 in credit) and four monthly points (2018-03-31, 2019-01-31, 2019-12-31, 2020-10-31).
- **Day period view (2026-07-20, phase 2; the global toolbar pair moved per-surface 2026-07-26 — see «Per-surface display controls»):** `metricsPeriod` is `"day" | "month" | "year"` (was toolbar Período: Diario/Mensual/Anual). Dashboard rows carry `deposits_day_* / delta_day_* / prior_day_close_*`, anchored at **plain yesterday (Chile calendar) for every account** (2026-07-21; the per-class `dayWindowAnchor.ts` machinery was deleted — marks are flat on each account's closed days, so per-account market calendars fall out of the level delta with no anchor table). Both liabilities keep the loss-negative convention `prior − close − payments` (CC `delta_day` = −that day's financing charges; see the CC financing-P/L bullet below). Crypto rows mark **live** — `getAccountPositionMeta`'s crypto branch is live-first like `equityBrokeragePositionMeta` (a raw-EOD position value pinned rows at yesterday's close and zeroed the day delta, since the calendar-day anchor mark IS that close). Bucket totals carry `prior_closes.day` + `day_end` (per-account anchors via `dashboardBucketDayPriorCloses`), and `dashboardNavCardMetrics` emits a `day` variant per node (inversiones hub composes day from child buckets — the consolidated slice is monthly). **Month-grain trap:** `buildDashboardBucketValueTotals` maps the consolidated *monthly* closing onto every date of its month — never use it for daily grids; per-session values come from `buildDashboardBucketDailySeriesClp` (Σ raw marks + cash_eqs CC netting), which also feeds `GET /api/dashboard/overview-daily` (`daily.overview|` cache keys) and the day prior-closes; the daily payload carries **`invested`** (retirement + brokerage level) and **`liabilities`** (Σ liability-account marks — mortgage balance + CC owed-on-date) per point (2026-07-22), with the monthly builder's leading-null convention for real_estate/liabilities, so the day-mode overview chart shows the same line set as monthly. Client: pickers three-way (`periodMetricsFromVariant`), month/year-only surfaces clamp via `monthYearMetricsPeriod` (flow pages), the overview chart swaps to the daily series (`primaryXAxisGranularity="day"`: densify/zero-anchor pass-through, month-boundary axis ticks, ISO tooltip titles), nav-snapshot cache bumped to v7.
- **ATH marker on the dashboard Patrimonio neto chart (2026-09-21; `netWorthAllTimeHigh.ts`, `AthMarker.tsx`, `athMarkerPlacement.ts`).** SERVER-computed, **one peak per grain, each over the series that grain plots**, so the diamond always sits on the line: `day` = the FULL-HISTORY daily net-worth walk (`buildDashboardBucketDailySeriesClp` over `portfolioStartYmd..today`, today live); `month` / `year` = the latest-dated overview point of each calendar month / year (`pickAllTimeHighOnCalendarGrid` — the same row-selection rules as the client's `densifyRecordsByCalendarPeriod` and `rollupTimeseriesBlockYearEnd`, so the three can differ in date and value: 2026-06-02 2xx.xxx.xxx daily vs June's 2xx.xxx.xxx monthly). Per display unit (each day at its own fx/UF; the CLP and USD peaks can be different days), ties resolve to the EARLIEST day. The daily peak rides the mark cache under the `daily.overview|ath|<unit>` key — deliberately under the `daily.overview|` prefix so a live-quote tick (`live_tail`) drops it too (today's live point can set a new high). Emitted as `overview.ath: {day, month, year}` on the dashboard valuation-timeseries / page-bundle payloads (`attachNetWorthAth`, decorating like `attachColorsToValuationPayload`; month/year read the block's own points) and as `ath` (day grain) on `/api/dashboard/overview-daily`; the keep-prev unit conversion drops it (a scaled CLP peak is not the USD peak) until the real bundle lands. Client: `DashboardPage` PICKS the grain matching the surface's Período (daily payload's `ath` in Diario, `overview.ath.month` / `.year` otherwise) and `LineChartPanel.athMarker` (only the home overview passes it) draws a small unlabeled diamond `ReferenceDot` on the plotted row of that date (resolved by day / month / year prefix — the yearly rollup re-dates rows to Dec 31), absent when the Rango clip leaves the row out of the window. The diamond takes no pointer events; instead the docked tooltip — which already tracks the cursor's x — appends an ATH footer line (diamond glyph, `charts.athTooltip` date framed like the title, value) while the hovered row is within `athTooltipIndexTolerance` of the peak's row (≈1,2% of the plotted rows, never less than the adjacent row — a dozen daily rows sit under one pixel-ish of cursor travel), bright on the peak's own row and muted on the neighbours.
- **Pending current bucket carries forward (2026-08-11):** `LineChartPanel` densify-extends every valuation chart's x-axis through Chile today, so when the day/month/year turned before fresh data loaded, the extension bucket rendered as a fabricated 0 on every line (the trailing-zero coercion flipped the synthetic all-null row) — the midnight plunge-to-zero on every page. Now `carryForwardTrailingPendingRows` (`chartDensifyTimeSeries.ts`) copies the last real row into trailing synthetic rows before `coerceKeptTrailingZeroMonth`, but only keys with a finite NON-ZERO value at the last real row — the server tail clip ends a sold-out series in one kept 0 (or nulls), so ended lines are never resurrected and the `[…, x, 0]` sold-out plot survives. Complement: `useChileDayRolloverInvalidation` (mounted in `AppTree`) watches `chileTodayYmd()` (60s interval + focus/visibilitychange) and invalidates ALL react-query caches on day rollover, so day-baked payloads refetch right after midnight instead of waiting out `staleTime`/`refetchInterval` (~5 min); server caches already clear on Chile-day rollover (`cacheRegistry.ts` day epoch), so the first post-midnight fetch is correct.
- **CC per-day owed + daily extras (2026-07-21, phase 4):** between stored anchors, `accountMarkClpAtYmd`'s CC branch returns anchor + **post-anchor signed line activity** (charges +, PAGOs −, `postCloseLiveBalanceAdjustmentClp` — the same evidence stream the month-end writer applies post-cierre). **Installment purchases ramp daily (2026-07-22):** the daily walk and the daily CC netting pass `includeInstallmentPurchases` — each contract adds its full `cc_installment_purchases.total_amount_clp` on its `purchase_date` (cupo is consumed at purchase, matching the printed «cupo utilizado en cuotas»), so cuota-heavy months ramp instead of stepping vertically at the next anchor; per-month cuota billing lines stay excluded (billing moves debt between facturado/por-facturar, it doesn't change owed) and anchors still snap to evidence. The month-end writer must NOT pass the flag — its `balance_total` is cupo-based at month-end and already contains post-cierre contracts (passing it double-counted them). The normalized line stream is memoized under the `cc.billing_detail|<id>|postclose_lines` satellite key (per-account `invalidateCcBillingDetail` prefix-deletes `<key>|`). **Header-only payments (2026-07-22):** the current Santander CLP format prints the previous facturación's payment as a dated «MONTO CANCELADO» row that the parser drops by design (the amount lives in the statement header) — so the stream synthesizes the PAGO event from `cc_statements.monto_pagado_anterior` + `monto_pagado_anterior_date` (migration 166; parser captures the printed date when exactly one body row matches the header amount). Statements whose payment exists as a real line are skipped (no double count); duplicate statement versions collapse on the shared `hdr-pago|` key. Without this the between-anchors walk only ever climbed (charges had lines, payments didn't). The cash_eqs linked-CC netting uses the same daily convention at historical dates (`liabilityTree.linkedCreditCardBalanceTotalClpAsOf`). **Range selector (2026-07-21, replaced the day-only Sesiones dropdown; per-surface since 2026-07-26)**: `Rango: 30d/60d/90d/6m/1y/3y/5y/10y/Todo` (`client/src/timeRange.ts`; localStorage `nw-tracker.timeRange`, `DisplayPreferencesContext.timeRange`, default `total`). Daily fetches send `days = timeRangeToDays(range)` (0 = total); **M/Y charts clip client-side** (a yearly chart cuts its months first, then rolls up — `clipMonthsThenRollup`, see «Yearly charts start with a partial first year») to the same range inside `LineChartPanel` + `MonthlyPerformanceComboChart` (`clipPointsToTimeRange`; when the clip removes leading history the initial zero anchors are skipped — no false left-edge dive to 0). **Agrupado daily**: `/api/daily-series` emits `grouped_accounts` (`groupDailySeriesAccounts` over the nav/liabilities `ChartBucketPlan` — synthetic ids match the monthly grouped blocks, so the client builder reuses that block's metadata); Pasivos pages (`LiabilitiesGroupPage`) swap to the daily chart too (per-card buckets). **Sin-agrupar daily (2026-07-26):** nav pages also get `ungrouped_accounts` + `ungrouped_proportional` from the `grouped: false` plan (one nav level deeper — on the Inversiones hub that's the 6 second-level buckets, matching the monthly toggle-off view), **only when that plan's bucket keys differ from the grouped plan's**; on leaf pages both plans are identical, so the client reuses `grouped_accounts` for the toggle-off view (absence-means-identical rule in `GroupInfoPage`'s three daily memos — valuation pairs with `nav_grouped_blocks.ungrouped`, P/L bars with `nav_grouped_bars.ungrouped`, which also fixed toggle-off daily bars reading 0 against synthetic bar ids). **CC chart window (2026-07-22; per chart since 2026-09-22):** the historial (on the card page AND the Pasivos / credit-card group pages) windows on its OWN Rango across all three period modes — `[max(range_cutoff, first_data − 0.2·span), max(today, plan_end)]` (`client/src/chartRangeWindow.ts`: `rangeWindowStartYmd` keeps a 20% empty lead before the data as a truncation cue and never reaches past the range, `total` starts flush; `windowMonthRows` left-clips the monthly rows AND pads empty leading months so M/Y match the dense daily grid's left edge — verified D↔M share `nov 2023 → dic 2027` on ·0781 at Rango 5y). The daily historial reads a server-built **CC block** (`ccDailyHistorialBlock.ts`, attached to `/api/daily-series` on CLP requests only — the chart is CLP-native and the client asks for clp): **`cc_owed`** (saldo total = Σ of the scope's CC-master lines, the per-day owed walk — never the bucket `points`, which on the Pasivos root include the mortgage), **`cc_installment_debt`** (Σ per master), **`cc_facturacion_bars`** (each card's facturación bar on its close date, see «Historial bars») and **`cc_plan_tail`** (`ccInstallmentPlanTailClpForAccounts` in `ccInstallmentDebtDaily.ts`) over `today+1 .. plan_end` (= the LATEST member's last cuota pay-by; every member with a schedule walks the shared grid, so a settled member's open-cycle carry still rides until its own pay-by and the today→tomorrow seam stays continuous), CLP-only. **Group scope (2026-09-22):** the Pasivos root and the credit-card group get the same block summed over the masters `resolveCcMasterAccountIds` gives the merged monthly ledger (`creditCardGroupLedger.ts`) — one membership for both grains; a card page passes its single id through the same builder. `plan_debt_clp` continues the daily `buildCcInstallmentDebtDailySeries` walk; `balance_clp` = plan debt + the unpaid non-installment carry (`owed_today − plan_debt_today` via `accountMarkClpAtYmd`, frozen at today) until it is paid, then the two lines coincide — **split by facturación (2026-09-26):** while the latest closed facturación (statement or provisional) is still ahead of its pay-by, the open cycle's own charges (`facturadoClpFromOpenMonthStatementLines` — tagged cuota purchases excluded, they ride in the plan debt — clamped to the carry) leave on the open pay-by and the rest on the closed one's (`closedFacturacionAheadOfPayBy`); a single drop at the open pay-by held September 2026's ~1,xx M of únicos until 10/11 instead of 10/10 — reproducing the monthly projected rows (`balance_total = cupo` once facturado is null) at each projected month-end within a small daily-vs-monthly rounding residue (~57 CLP on ·0781; the daily cupo line already carries that basis, so the tail stays internally consistent). Daily mode marks `today` with a dashed «Hoy (proyección →)» reference line where the real owed walk hands off to the projection.
- **Traspaso de deuda links (2026-08-05):** a «traspaso de deuda internacional» reclassifies USD debt onto the CLP side of the same card — the USD statement's abono leg and the CLP statement's cargo leg of one facturación are a single event, no cash moves, total debt unchanged. **`cc_traspaso_deuda_links`** (migration 170) stores the pair with the bank's hard conversion (implied rate = `amount_clp / amount_usd`); `normalizedPostCloseLines` values a linked USD leg at **minus the CLP leg's booked pesos**, so the pair nets to exactly zero in the owed walk / daily CC netting / flow legs instead of leaving an fx residue (was ±10k CLP per traspaso window). Links are **derived state, rebuilt per account inside every PDF statement import** (`relinkCcTraspasoDeudaLinksForAccount` in `ccTraspasoDeudaLinks.ts`; statement-line replacement cascades old rows away) and the relink **throws on an unpairable leg** — one CLP + one USD leg per statement close, signs validated, rate sanity band — so importing a facturación with only one of its two statement currencies fails loudly when a traspaso is present. Web-paste bucket lines never participate (a pasted leg can legitimately arrive alone; it keeps today's fx conversion until the PDF import links the real pair). Manual re-check/backfill: `npx tsx server/scripts/relink-cc-traspaso-deuda.ts [--apply]`.
- **Strip 1D/1W + ticker displayPL (2026-07-21):** the Rentabilidad short-horizon cells are calendar-framed — 1D = today (live) vs **yesterday**, 1W = vs 7 calendar days ago (`periodReturnsShortHorizon.ts`; the NYSE display-session framing and `d1_is_live` are gone — MTD keeps its live badge). The 1D cell equals the daily table's last row by construction, every day incl. weekends. Money surfaces (strip, dashboard day cells, daily table, bucket cards) always show **real** calendar-day PL; per-instrument day changes (marquee, watchlist 1D, RN-proxy composite) go through **`displayDayPct`** (`tickerDayDisplay.ts`) — hard 0 on the instrument's closed days (NYSE calendar for USD tickers, Chile for `.SN`/fund cuotas, weekends for fx; crypto/UF always real). Tickers only: a US-holiday fx drift on a USD stock stays visible as real P/L in money surfaces while its marquee chip reads 0. **Marquee and watchlist follow the CLP/USD toggle (2026-09-21):** `GET /api/market-ticker?unit=` and `GET /api/watchlist?unit=` (the query key carries the unit; `watchlistDisplayUnitParam`) express every value AND every change column in the display unit, converted leg by leg in `watchlistStatsForRow` — the current value at the fx of its observation day (live while that fx day is open), each anchor at the fx on or before the anchor's own date (`watchlistAnchors.ts`, shared with the composite proxy), the day anchor at the fx of the calendar day before the observation — so in CLP mode a USD ticker's chip is price × fx and equals the account row's day P/L (VEA read +1,32% on the strip against +0,05% in the table on 2026-09-21: the peso had rallied 1,26%). A series already quoted in the requested unit is untouched (CLP-mode numbers for CLP series are the pre-toggle ones); the USD/CLP row is never converted (it is the rate, CLP per USD in both modes), nor are the other rate rows (kind `fx_pair`, migration 313, `series_key` `eur_usd` / `eur_clp`, read through `pairRateOnOrBefore` in `rates.ts`: quote units per one base unit, on the marquee as the payload's `fx_pairs` and on the rates page as `/api/market-series`'s `eur_usd`, 2026-11-12); the marquee payload is built from the same row stats (`uf`/`uno_a`/`risky_norris`/`risky_norris_proxy` = `{day, value, currency, delta_pct}`, equities `{value, currency}` = the display unit), so strip and page cannot disagree. The closed-day hard 0 stays keyed on the instrument's calendar.
- **Group/account daily views (phase 3):** `GET /api/daily-series?portfolio_group=|account_id=&unit=&days=` serves the bucket daily series **plus per-account chart lines** (`getBucketDailySeries({ includeAccounts })` — same marks, zero extra cost; group scope resolves via `listAccountsForGroupTab`, so the Pasivos tabs work too). In day mode the group/account valuation chart swaps to those lines (`buildDailyValuationBlock` in `client/src/dailySeriesChart.ts` reuses the monthly block's account metadata so series identities/colors survive the M↔D toggle; Agrupado stays monthly-only), including the **aportes acum. companions** (`deposits_acum` per account + `deposits_acum_total` = full-history cumulative deposits at each day, same event source as the flow legs) — the Aportes acumulados toggle behaves identically to the monthly view and the detalle table becomes **`DailyPerfDetailTable`** (fecha ISO / cierre / aportes día / P/L día / % día; desktop `<td>` + `TableMobileCard` in sync). The last daily row equals the Rentabilidad 1D cell by construction (shared legs).
- **Chart-host reference overlays in day mode (2026-07-23):** the Pasivos «Disponible»/«Disponible total» and Efectivo «Tarjeta de crédito» lines are weighted sums of OTHER nav groups (`portfolio_groups.group_kind = 'reference'` + `linked_group` items — data-driven, never a slug list), so the daily payload carries **`reference_lines: [{dataKey, values}]`** built by `dailyReferenceLinesForChartHost` (`server/src/dailyReferenceLines.ts`) and the client pairs them with the monthly block's `lines` metadata (same borrow-the-metadata rule as `grouped_accounts`; unknown dataKey → skipped, never a synthesized label). Both paths pick the host through one helper, `chartHostSlugForValuationGroup`, so monthly and daily can't drift. Source totals are read **on-or-before**: `groupTabValuationTotalFromBuilt` keeps every point of the source's own chart and `forwardFillTotalsToChartDates` picks per host date — it used to intersect with the host's dates first, which dropped a source's mid-month point (Fintual/APV snapshots land on flow days) and carried the previous month, so the monthly overlay read a month stale against the daily one (APV 2026-06: 1,xx M on «Disponible total»). **A cash source must be CC-netted** (`linkedCreditCardClpForCashCardByDates`): the monthly chart Total for cash_eqs comes from the consolidated cierre via `applyConsolidatedTotalToGroupTabBlock`, which nets the linked card — without it the daily overlay sits a whole card balance (~10M) above the monthly one. **Each source reads that group's OWN daily series** — `getBucketDailySeriesCached` under the same scope key, rows and options the `/api/daily-series` group branch uses (`pg:<slug>`, `includeAccounts: true`, rows filtered only by `account_id > 0`), so one build serves both and opening Pasivos warms Brokerage/Efectivo/APV in day mode (and vice versa). Keep those three inputs identical or the key silently stops matching and both sides rebuild. A thin per-unit `daily.refsrc|<slug>|<unit>|<days>` map caches the netted result on top (the CC netting is not free to redo per request); everything drops under the `daily.` prefix. Reference sources must resolve by **raw slug** (the daily route's convention, checked equal to the monthly `portfolioGroupApiForValuation` sets for all four sources). **Cost** (full history, fresh process): Pasivos cold 14,1s → 15,3s with sharing, but the three source pages go 3,5s/1,9s/1,2s → 27ms/412ms/13ms — a session that visits them is ~5s faster overall; warm ~30ms. Marks dominate a daily build, so a values-only source build saves little; the remaining idea is caching the per-account marks themselves so the flow legs are all a group page adds. Expected D↔M gap: the monthly composer only reads source rows whose date is a chart date and forward-fills the rest, so a month-end where a source has no row (e.g. APV 2026-06) shows a stale monthly value while the daily marks the real one.

## Equity / crypto tickers (`accounts.equity_ticker`)

- Yahoo symbols (e.g. `SPY`, `OILK`, `BTC-USD`, `CFIETFIPSA.SN`) live on **`accounts.equity_ticker`**, set at **panel stock create** (historically by the retired excel import) — not parsed from notes or asset-group slug at runtime.
- **The Ether and Bitcoin ledgers come from Buda's own history (2026-09-27; `server/scripts/rebuild-crypto-ledger-from-buda.ts`, report-first, `--apply`).** It replaced the rows the retired excel importer wrote from the «cripto-*» sheets (month-end dates, running totals misread, true-ups forced onto the sheet's footer — the Ether balance went negative for months and read as a one-day +94%). Inputs: `cfraser/buda-history.csv` plus the untracked `cfraser/buda-ledger-facts.json` (fee model, monthly pins, withdrawal fees, the coin swap, sell-alls, round trips). One single-leg row per Buda event, paired 1:1 with the untouched Buda CLP buffer's rows (same day, same pesos), so trades net to zero inside the crypto bucket; a coin sent to another exchange that came back later (a round trip) stays held and marked at market, and only its send fees are lost. Unit-only rows carry amount 0 with `cash_fee` (coin lost) / `savings_earnings` (coin gained). The coin accounts are out of both deposit-matcher pools (`loadCryptoCoinAccountIdsFundedByBuda`), so the checking debit behind an abono goes to the abono, not to the same-day coin buy it funded; `syncBudaAbonoDepositMirrors` mirrors only abonos with no real outflow link (`listBudaAbonosWithoutRealOutflowLink`) and keeps a needed mirror's row id; synthetic mirror lines skip the manual-assertion pass (they already carry their direct link). Repair of pre-fix links: `server/scripts/remove-redundant-deposit-mirror-links.ts` (report-first, `--apply`). **The deposit matcher claims each deposit once and prefers dated counterparts (2026-09-28):** the deposit pool leaves out a transfer leg whose other endpoint is a checking account (`depositEventExplainedByCheckingTransfer` in `checkingCartolaLoaders.ts`, via `loadMergedDepositInflowEventsBankDatedWithTransferCounter` — the transfer row IS that outflow, and a leg has no movement row to link, so a same-amount debit that paired with it left its real deposit unexplained); a month-precision deposit (cuenta de ahorro) is only a fallback — an exact-date internal deposit wins, and an investment deposit of the full amount within the day window blocks the month match (`findInternalCashTransferDepositPreferringExactDays`); and the checking accounts' outflows are matched in date order across the accounts with one `usedDepositKeys` set (`buildCheckingGastosLinesForAccounts`), so two debits no longer both claim one deposit and the earlier debit claims first whichever account it sits on (account order had handed a month-precision deposit to corriente just because it runs first). An ahorro split's mirror carries only the self-funded pesos no real (non-mirror) link already explains (`syncCuentaAhorroDepositSplitMirrors`, the Buda rule). The matcher only writes its `auto:deposit-match` note when the debit has no user-assigned category, so Buda-era wires still carrying excel-era spending categories kept mirrors: `server/scripts/archive/link-buda-abonos-behind-stale-categories.ts` (report-first, `--apply`, IMMEDIATE) sets those categories to `deposits` when exactly one debit of the same pesos sits within three days of an unlinked abono, and throws unless each abono ends linked to its debit. **A claim is one deposit event, never account + day + pesos (2026-09-28):** every candidate carries `claim_key` (`<account>|<event key>` from the merged deposit timeline — `m:<id>` for a movement row, `t:<id>:<endpoint>` for a transfer leg) and `movement_id`, so twin deposits (same account, day and pesos — ahorro splits entered twice) are two claims two debits can take, and one debit still takes one; the splittable reserva pool is keyed the same way. The `auto:deposit-match` segment names the claimed row (`…|mov:<id>`) and the link sync links that row (`findDepositMovementForSegment`; a segment without it — notes written before — still resolves by account, day and pesos, and skips twins as ambiguous). The cuenta vista's credits, loaded both as a deposit account and as an internal-transfer target, collapse to one candidate per claim key (a repeat with different fields throws). **A checking credit is an internal arrival only from the client's own traspaso (2026-10-31; `checking_own_traspaso` on the candidate, `isCheckingOwnTraspasoInflow` / `isCheckingOwnTraspasoOutflow`):** a credit on a checking account counts only when the bank printed it as a traspaso from the other checking account («desde Cta.Ct», «de Cuenta Vista»), and only a debit printed as an own traspaso («a Cuentamática», «a Cuenta Vista…», «a Cta. Cte.») may claim it; a «Transf. Internet a otro Bancos» left the bank. Twelve wires to people at other banks had paired with third parties' transfers into checking, or with the real traspaso of the next day, and read as internal transfers. **Monthly perf carries cumulative deposits through filler months** (2026-09-27): a month with no valuation row keeps the previous cumulative deposits in `buildAccountMonthlyPerformanceUncached` (reading it as 0 split one flow into a ± couplet across the gap).
- **Crypto movement kinds are structured state (2026-09-29; migration 195, `cryptoMovementKinds.ts`).** `crypto_movement_kinds` holds each coin row's kind (buy, sell, swap_out, swap_in, coin_out, send_fee, round_trip_return) — the Buda rebuild had written it only into the note. It was filled by `server/scripts/backfill-crypto-movement-kinds.ts` from the ledger's structure alone (`deduceCryptoMovementKinds`: the fee/return flow kinds, a buy or sell pairs with the opposite peso row of the same day in the Buda CLP buffer, an unpaired coin-out/coin-in of the same pesos on two coins the same day is a swap, any other coin-out is a coin_out, any other coin-in throws), checked once against the notes (63 of 63 agree); `rebuild-crypto-ledger-from-buda.ts` writes it with every row. Read it with `loadCryptoMovementKinds` (throws on a row without one). Tax lots: `taxLots.ts` (`realizeTaxLots`, FIFO / LIFO / average or a custom selector), fed by `equityTaxLotEvents.ts` and `cryptoTaxLotEvents.ts` (`CRYPTO_KIND_LOT_TREATMENT` says how each kind counts). The dollars on the USD cash accounts are lots too (`usdCashTaxLotEvents.ts`: units = dollars, pesos at the dólar observado of each day, `observadoOnOrBefore` carrying weekends up to 7 days): the posture decides when the exchange result counts — `oficio_2573` (dollars spent on a `stock_buy` are sold, Oficio 2573/2022), `oficio_2390` (only on reconversion to pesos) or `none` — and tags each disposal `realized` / `deferred`. **Crypto commissions come from the exchange's own record (2026-09-30; migration 197, `cryptoTradeDetails.ts`):** `crypto_trade_details` holds each buy / sell / swap_in trade's units, price and commission as Buda's transaction panel prints them (`server/scripts/import-buda-trade-details.ts`, pairing every trade 1:1 with its ledger row by kind, coin, Chile day and pesos within 2; the raw capture lives in `cfraser/`). The SII does not let a commission reduce a crypto gain (Oficio 2208/2022), so the crypto lots default to `excluded`: a purchase costs what was paid less its commission, a sale earns what was received plus it — the 2025 gross sales and purchase then equal what Buda reported in DJ 1964 to the peso. `included` keeps the ledger's pesos. The Buda rebuild's modeled coin fees left 2017 buy units up to 0,00025 ETH off Buda's net units; the pesos are exact.
- **Crypto has no stored valuations (2026-09-28).** A crypto account (`accountUsesCryptoMtm`) is valued from units × `equity_daily` close × fx on every surface; `crypto:apply-valuation` (which rewrote month-end `valuations` rows, the current month-end among them, ahead of today) is gone, and the stored-row fallbacks in `accountMarkClpAtYmd`, `valuationRawClpForAccount`, `monthEndCloseForPerformance`, the position meta and `syncLatestDisplayValueClp` now throw through `requireCryptoMtmClp` when the close or fx is missing (backfill the series). The chart grid (`snapshotDatesForCryptoAccount`) is movement month-ends + the coin series' month-ends + today, never later than the current month-end, and `POST /api/accounts/:id/valuations` refuses a crypto account. Verified byte-transparent on a real-DB copy (every dashboard, group, account, daily and flows payload identical with the rows deleted); the rows go with `server/scripts/archive/delete-redundant-crypto-valuations.ts` (report-first, `--apply`; refuses non-crypto accounts).
- **Manual watchlist symbols are checked against Yahoo before they are added (2026-10-04; migration 210, `marketSymbols.ts`, `yahooSymbols.ts`).** The add box is a combobox over Yahoo's search (`GET /api/watchlist/search?q=`), and `POST /api/watchlist` asks Yahoo's chart for the symbol first: no price (Yahoo's `DXY` is a dead Nasdaq stub — the dollar index is `DX-Y.NYB`), a currency the `currencies` table does not hold (pence, `GBp`, refused by name), or a market other than New York / Santiago / London / crypto is refused. These two are the only Yahoo calls on a request path, both the user's own action; display reads stay DB-only. What Yahoo said is recorded in `market_symbols` (quote currency, market calendar) and read by `marketQuoteCurrency` / `equityMarketKind` before the suffix rule. **An index has no quote currency** (migration 315; it was the string `none` until then): its level is points, stored as a NULL `market_symbols.quote_currency` / `equity_daily.currency` / `live_market_quotes.currency` (null in memory, `MarketQuoteCurrency = string | null`), shown unconverted in every display unit (on the wire still `value_currency: "none"` / chip `currency: "none"`, so the client is unchanged; marquee chip without a currency sign), left out of the rates-page market series (`currency IS NOT NULL`), and `equityQuoteCurrency` (holdings, trades, dividends, composites) throws on it. Each Yahoo failure of a watchlist symbol (live quote, history backfill) is kept in `market_symbol_fetch_errors` until the next success and shown under the row's symbol (`fetch_error`); the history backfill no longer stops at the first failing ticker. NYSE-calendar daily bars dated on a weekend are dropped at parse (the ICE index opens Sunday evening).
- **One account per ticker, whatever the broker (2026-10-06; `equityBrokerHoldings.ts`).** Every importer resolves a stock by `accountIdForEquityTicker` (exactly one account per ticker, else it throws), so the same symbol bought at Racional, Fintual or a later broker lands on one account; the broker is only the cash leg of each trade. The stock page's position carries `brokers`: the shares grouped by the cash account on the other side (`from` of a buy, `to` of a sale; a single-leg units row has none), checked to add up to the position, with value = position value × share. The page shows it only when two or more cash accounts hold the ticker.
- Read via `equityTickerForAccount` / `requireEquityTicker` in `server/src/accountEquityTicker.ts`. Equity-MTM accounts without a ticker should throw (fix the `accounts.equity_ticker` data; the one-time backfill migration 089 was squashed into the schema baseline).
- Marquee / stock EOD sync: `listDistinctEquityTickersForSync()` / `listWatchlistStockTickersForEodSync()` from DB. Account identity lives in `accounts.import_key` (`import:excel|key=…`, `import:panel|…`); `notes` is human provenance.
- **Watchlist multi-year anchors (3y/5y/10y):** the scheduler-tick backfill (`ensureWatchlistEquityHistoryDepth`, live-quotes tick — request paths never fetch) only reaches ~400 days. Deeper history for all watchlist equity/crypto tickers (top-level + RN proxy constituents, `.SN` skipped): **`npm run backfill:watchlist-equity-history -w nw-tracker-server`** (default 11y Yahoo; `--years N`, `--dry-run`; drops the in-progress UTC day for crypto so only completed EOD is stored). Ran 2026-07-03 (Yahoo depth reaches ~2015-06). UF / FX history for the same anchors: `npm run backfill:bcentral-uf` (BCentral, needs `BCENTRAL_*` creds) and `npm run backfill:yahoo-fx-usd` — both fill from `portfolioStartYmd` (earliest movement/valuation = 2016-05-31; override with `PORTFOLIO_START_YMD=YYYY-MM-DD`). Re-ran 2026-07-13 with `PORTFOLIO_START_YMD=2015-05-01` (plus `backfill:sbif-fx-eur`) so `fx_daily` / `fx_daily_bcentral` / `eur_daily` / `uf_daily` cover the full ~11y equity window (`equity_daily` starts 2015-06-25).

- **Stock splits (2026-11-05; migration 265, `equitySplits.ts`).** `equity_splits` (ticker, ex-date, numerator : denominator, source `yahoo` | `broker` | `manual`, write-once; another ratio for the same ex-date throws). Two frames: **prices are split-adjusted** — `equity_daily`, `equity_dividends` and `live_market_quotes` hold every price in today's share terms (Yahoo's convention), so return readers (watchlist, benchmarks, proxy basket, day changes) read them as stored; **shares are as held** — the split is a `stock_split` movement on each holder on the ex-date (single-leg, amount 0, `units_delta` = shares the day before × (ratio − 1); in `BROKERAGE_SHARE_UNITS_FLOW_KINDS`, out of deposits). Valuing the shares held on a day reads the price as traded that day, `equityAsTradedClose` = adjusted close × the ratios of later splits (`computeEquityMtmClp`, `holdingPriceAt` for stocks, the position meta). `recordEquitySplit` does it all in one transaction: the split row, the rescale of every stored price before the ex-date (closes walked newest first against their post-split neighbour, so rows a source had already adjusted are kept), each holder's split row; it refuses an ex-date after Chile today.
